---
title: "google_aiplatform_evaluation_item"
description: "`google_aiplatform_evaluation_item` is a single scored case (one input, its generated output..."
---

`google_aiplatform_evaluation_item` is a single scored case (one input, its generated output, and its evaluation result) produced as part of a Vertex AI Gen AI Evaluation Service evaluation run.

## Example

<Tabs>
  <Tab title="Go">
    ```go
    package main

    import (
    	ubx "github.com/ubiquex/ubx-sdk-go/runtime"
    	aiplatform "github.com/ubiquex/ubx-sdk-google/sdk/go/google/aiplatform"
    )

    func main() {
    	ubx.Main(ubx.Stack("example", func() {
    		ubx.Intent(ubx.IntentInfo{Summary: "example own evaluation item"})

    		ubx.Resource(aiplatform.EvaluationItem, "example", aiplatform.EvaluationItemConfig{
    			Name: "example",
    		})
    	}))
    }
    ```
  </Tab>
  <Tab title="TypeScript">
    ```typescript
    import { intent, resource, stack } from "@ubx/sdk";
    import { EvaluationItem } from "@ubx/sdk-google/google/aiplatform/evaluation_item";

    export default stack("example", () => {
      intent({ summary: "example own evaluation item" });

      resource(EvaluationItem, "example", {
        name: "example",
      });
    });
    ```
  </Tab>
  <Tab title="Python">
    ```python
    import ubx_sdk as ubx
    from ubx.google.aiplatform import EvaluationItem, EvaluationItemConfig

    def describe():
        ubx.intent("example own evaluation item")

        ubx.resource(EvaluationItem, "example", EvaluationItemConfig(
            name="example",
        ))

    if __name__ == "__main__":
        ubx.run("example", describe)
    ```
  </Tab>
  <Tab title="Markdown">
    ```
    Create google_aiplatform_evaluation_item called "example", with .
    ```
  </Tab>
</Tabs>

## Properties

<ResponseField name="create_time" type="string">
  Output only. Timestamp when this item was created. Optional; if omitted, computed by Google Cloud.
</ResponseField>

<ResponseField name="display_name" type="string">
  Required. The display name of the EvaluationItem. Optional; if omitted, computed by Google Cloud.
</ResponseField>

<ResponseField name="error" type="object">
  The `Status` type defines a logical error model that is suitable for different programming environments, including REST APIs and RPC APIs. It is used by [gRPC](https://github.com/grpc). Each `Status` message contains three pieces of data: error code, error message, and error details. You can find out more about this error model and how to work with it in the [API Design Guide](https://cloud.google.com/apis/design/errors). Optional; if omitted, computed by Google Cloud.
  <Expandable title="properties">
    <ResponseField name="code" type="number">
      The status code, which should be an enum value of google.rpc.Code. Optional.
    </ResponseField>
    <ResponseField name="details" type="list(map(any))">
      A list of messages that carry the error details. There is a common set of message types for APIs to use. Optional.
    </ResponseField>
    <ResponseField name="message" type="string">
      A developer-facing error message, which should be in English. Any user-facing error message should be localized and sent in the google.rpc.Status.details field, or localized by the client. Optional.
    </ResponseField>
  </Expandable>
</ResponseField>

<ResponseField name="evaluation_item_type" type="string">
  Required. The type of the EvaluationItem. Optional; if omitted, computed by Google Cloud.
</ResponseField>

<ResponseField name="evaluation_request" type="object">
  A single evaluation request supporting input for both single-turn model generation and multi-turn agent execution traces. Valid input modes: 1. Inference Mode: `prompt` is set (containing text or AgentData context). 2. Offline Eval Mode: `prompt` is unset, and `candidate_responses` contains `agent_data` (the completed execution trace). Validation Rule: Either `prompt` must be set, OR at least one of the `candidate_responses` must contain `agent_data`. Optional; if omitted, computed by Google Cloud.
  <Expandable title="properties">
    <ResponseField name="candidate_responses" type="list(object)">
      Optional. Responses from model under test and other baseline models for comparison. Optional.
      <Expandable title="properties">
        <ResponseField name="agent_data" type="object">
          The agent's own recorded state and turn history for this evaluation candidate. Optional; if omitted, computed by Google Cloud. **(AI-inferred)**
          <Expandable title="properties">
            <ResponseField name="agents" type="map(object)">
              The agent(s) involved in this evaluation, keyed by their own identifier. Optional; if omitted, computed by Google Cloud. **(AI-inferred)**
              <Expandable title="properties">

                  The identifier of the agent this applies to. Optional; if omitted, computed by Google Cloud. **(AI-inferred)**
                </ResponseField>
                <ResponseField name="agent_type" type="string">
                  The kind of agent this is, e.g. which framework or platform it's built on. Optional; if omitted, computed by Google Cloud. **(AI-inferred)**

                  A human-readable explanation of this schema's own meaning and intended use. Optional; if omitted, computed by Google Cloud. **(AI-inferred)**
